Implementation:
def build_U(h):
    n=len(h); L=2*n-1
    U=np.zeros((n,L), dtype=float)
    rev_one = np.ascontiguousarray((1.0 - h)[::-1])
    neg_h = -h
    for r in range(n):
        U[r, r:r+n] = rev_one
        U[r, n-1-r:n-r] = neg_h # since n-1-r + n = 2n-1-r? Wait end = start+n = n-1-r+n = 2n-1-r. Need slice end exclusive. For r=0 start n-1, end 2n-1? but L=2n-1, last index 2n-2. If start=n-1, length n => indices n-1 ..2n-2, end=2n-1. Formula end=n-1-r+n =2n-1-r. For r=0 2n-1 ok. For r=n-1 start0 end=n ok. So U[r, n-1-r:2n-1-r] = neg_h.
But careful slices if overlap with A; in overlapping part (where p=r and q=r same term? k=0 idx=n-1) A and B both should add. Slices overlap at idx =? A interval [r, r+n-1], B [n-1-r, 2n-2-r].     tmp[idx0] -= 2*amt*amt
    idx_ij = n-1 + i - j; tmp[idx_ij] += amt*amt
    idx_ji = n-1 + j - i; if idx_ji != idx_ij: tmp[...] += amt*amt
    assign c = tmp (or copy to preallocated)
    # h
    h[i]+=amt; h[j]-=amt

       U[r, ii1] -= amt
       if ii2 != ii1: U[r, ii2] -= amt
    for j decreased (d=-amt): changes +amt at indices
       jj1=n-1+r-j; jj2=n-1+j-r
       U[r,jj1] += amt
       if jj2 != jj1: U[r,jj2] += amt

def eval_move(c, U, i, j, amt, temp, diff):
    # returns new_max (unscaled)
    np.subtract(U[i], U[j], out=diff)
    np.multiply(diff, amt, out=diff) # in place
    np.add(c, diff, out=temp)
    amt2=amt*amt
    temp[idx0] -= 2*amt2
    idxij = idx[i,j] precompute? We can compute on fly n_minus1+i-j.
    temp[idxij] += amt2
    idxji = n_minus1+j-i
    if idxji != idxij: temp[idxji] += amt2
    return float(np.max(temp))

def project_feasible(h,n):

def project_feasible(h,n):
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  target=n/2.0
  for _ in range(1000):
    s=h.sum(); diff=target-s
    if abs(diff)<=1e-14: break
    if diff>0:
       mask=h<1.0
       if not mask.any(): break
       room=(1.0-h[mask]).sum()
       if room < 1e-15: break
       # To be exact, can scale up h[mask] by factor but stay <=1. Need find λ s.t. sum h_mask + λ*room=diff, 0≤λ≤1.
       lam=diff/room
       if lam>1: lam=1.0
       h[mask]=np.minimum(1.0, h[mask] + lam*room? Wait previous method adds (diff/room)*(1-h) => scales remaining room linearly; sum increase diff. That is exact if lam≤1. h[mask] += (diff/room)*(1-h[mask])? This increases each by fraction of its room proportional to room; sum increase diff. Good. If lam=1, h mask all to 1; sum max. So h[mask] += (diff/room)*(1.0 - h[mask]). This is exact and keeps <=1 ( floating). Good.
    else:
       mask=h>0
       tot=h[mask].sum()
       if tot <1e-15: break
       h[mask] *= (target/tot) if target < tot? Sum decrease. To keep >=0, factor=target/tot ≤1. exact.
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  # final adjust one fractional entry to fix residual
  diff=target-h.sum()
  if diff>0:
    idx=np.argsort(1-h)[::-1?] choose index with most room, add min(diff,1-h[idx])
  elif diff<0:
    idx choose max h, subtract min(-diff,h[idx])
  But modifying one entry could break exact if residual > room? residual should tiny. Could do loop. Return h.

Let's design local_polish with time.
Parameters: deadline, max_base=0.2? start base=0.05; base_factors. It will run until deadline- reserve. It returns h,c,U,best_C.
Pseudo:
def local_polish(h, c, U, deadline, dbg=False):
    n=len(h); dx=2.0/n
    best_cmax=float(c.max()); best_C=best_cmax*dx
    temp=np.empty(2*n-1); diff=np.empty(2*n-1)
    rows=np.arange(n); idx0=n-1
    base=0.05
    consecutive_no=0
    while time.time() < deadline and base >= 1e-7:
        move = scan_pairs(h,c,U,best_cmax,base,n,2*n-1,temp,diff,deadline)
        if move is None:
            consecutive_no += 1
            if base < 1e-5 and consecutive_no >= 2: break
            base *= 0.5
            continue
        i,j,amt = move
        # Optional line search for this direction
        # Determine sign and maxd from current h at i,j
        if amt > 0:
            sign = 1; maxd = min(1.0-h[i], h[j])
        else:
            sign = -1; maxd = min(1.0-h[j], h[i])
        if maxd <= 1e-12: continue
        # If amt not already at boundary and improvement significant, golden search
        # But scan moved maybe from probe. Use if time enough: if deadline - now > 0.5.
        # Could always golden (35 evals) overhead okay per move.
        t, val = golden_for_dir(... maxd, sign)
        # also compare with probe val? scan returned best probe but not value? return val too.
        # Use better of probe and golden.
        amt = sign * best_t
        # clamp
        if val < best_cmax - 1e-12:
             actual_amt = clamp
             apply_move
             best_cmax = c.max() # or val after apply maybe c changed; due rounding use c.max
             best_C = best_cmax*dx
             # occasionally recompute c,U? To avoid drift if many moves: after 1000 moves or every 10s? Could recompute if needed.
             move_count +=1
             if move_count % 200 ==0:
                 # recompute c=corr_full(h), U=build_U(h) maybe expensive O(n^2) but small. This ensures exact.
                 c = corr_full(h); U = build_U(h); best_cmax = c.max()
        consecutive_no=0
    return h,c,U,best_C

scan_pairs implementation:
def scan_pairs(h, c, U, best_cmax, base, n, L, temp, diff, deadline):
    tol=1e-13
    mini=1e-10
    best_val=best_cmax
    bi=-1; bj=-1; bam=0.0
    # tiny thresholds
    tiny=1e-5
    small=base*0.2
    for i in range(n-1):
        if time.time() > deadline: return None
        hi=h[i]
        # positive direction: i inc, j dec
        maxp_limit = 1.0 - hi
        for j in range(i+1,n):
            hj=h[j]
            maxp = hj if hj < maxp_limit else maxp_limit
            if maxp > mini:
                # evals helper inline
                am = maxp
                v=eval_move(...,am)
                if v < best_val - tol: best_val=v; bi=i;bj=j;ban=am
                am = 0.5*maxp
                if am > mini and am < am_prev - 1e-14? ...
            # negative direction: j inc, i dec => amt = -am, bounds maxp_neg = min(1-hj, hi)
    if bi<0: return None
    return (bi,bj,ban,best_val)

Eval_direction code:
def eval_direction(c,U,i,j,sign,maxd,base,n,temp,diff):
    mini=1e-10
    best_val = float('inf')
    best_t = 0.0
    prev = -1.0
    # frac 1,0.5,0.1
    for frac in EVAL_FRACTIONS:
       t = maxd * frac
       if t < mini: break? Since fractions descending, if t<mini break.
       if abs(t-prev) < 1e-15: continue
       v = eval_move(c,U,i,j,sign*t,n,temp,diff)
       if v < best_val:
           best_val=v; best_t=t; prev=t
       else:
           # still update prev? If t smaller, prev should track last evaluated t to avoid duplicates? Duplicates only if base close.
           pass
    # base_abs
    t = base if base < maxd else maxd
    if t > mini and abs(t-prev) > 1e-15:
       v=...
    # base_small
    bs=base*0.2
    t = bs if bs < maxd else maxd
    if t>mini ...
    # tiny
    t = 1e-5 if 1e-5 < maxd else maxd
    if t>mini ...
    if best_t==0.0: return 0.0, float('inf')
    return sign*best_t, best_val

Clamp amt before apply:
def clamp_move_amt(h,i,j,amt):
   if amt > 0:
      mx = min(1.0-h[i], h[j])
      if amt > mx: amt=mx
      if amt < 0: amt=0.0
   else:
      mx = min(1.0-h[j], h[i])
      amt = -amt
      if amt > mx: amt=mx
      amt = -amt
   if abs(amt) < 1e-15: return 0.0
   return amt

Now, U update vectorized after h update:
def update_U_after(P, p, delta, rows, idxA, idxB, n):
    if delta == 0: return
    U[rows, idxA[p]] -= delta
    U[rows, idxB[p]] -= delta

Function top-level:
def apply_move(c,U,h,i,j,amt,rows,idxA,idxB,n):
    if amt ==0: return
    L=2*n-1; idx0=n-1
    # c update
    c += amt * (U[i] - U[j])
    amt2=amt*amt
    c[idx0] -= 2*amt2
    idxij = idx0 + i - j
    c[idxij] += amt2
    idxji = idx0 + j - i
    if idxji != idxij:
       c[idxji] += amt2
    # h update
    hi = h[i] + amt
    hj = h[j] - amt
    # tiny clip
    if hi < 0: hi=0.0
    elif hi > 1: hi=1.0
    if hj < 0: hj=0.0
    elif hj > 1: hj=1.0
    h[i]=hi; h[j]=hj
    # U update
    # delta h_i = amt, h_j = -amt
    U[rows, idxA[i]] -= amt
    U[rows, idxB[i]] -= amt
    U[rows, idxA[j]] += amt
    U[rows, idxB[j]] += amt
    return
But if we clip hi/hj not equal to amt, c/U inconsistent. We clamped amt to avoid, but still rounding. We can not clip in apply, assume within; then after local_polish runs, project_feasible and recompute c/U. Since clip only in final, no incremental drift. But if amt causes h[i]=1+1e-17, U update still correct for delta; future h values outside tiny. Could cause bound violations in final. We clamp in project. We can in apply allow tiny outside and not clip; project_feasible at end. But local search bound checks use h[i] values; if h_i=1+eps, maxp negative maybe. - Resampling:
def resample_h(h, n_new):
   n_old=len(h); if n_new==n_old return h.copy()
   x_old = (np.arange(n_old)+0.5)*(2.0/n_old)
   x_new = (np.arange(n_new)+0.5)*(2.0/n_new)
   h_new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)
   return project_feasible(h_new, n_new)

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100):
  t0=time.time(); global_deadline=t0+budget_s-5
  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
  load initial.

  # Precompute rows, idx? local_polish builds.

  # Binary

def eval_move(c,U,i,j,amt,n,temp,diff):
   np.subtract(U[i], U[j], out=diff)
   diff *= amt
   np.add(c, diff, out=temp)
   a2=amt*amt
   temp[n-1] -= 2*a2
   idxij=n-1+i-j; temp[idxij]+=a2
   idxji=n-1+j-i
   if idxji != idxij: temp[idxji]+=a2
   return float(np.max(temp))

Now, project_feasible exact. Need for initial and final. Let's implement robust:
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.array(h,dtype=float).copy()
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    target=n/2.0
    for _ in range(1000):
        s=h.sum()
        diff=target-s
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            mask = h < 1.0
            if not np.any(mask): break
            room = (1.0 - h[mask]).sum()
            if room < 1e-15: break
            h[mask] += (diff/room)*(1.0 - h[mask])
        else:
            mask = h > 0.0
            if not np.any(mask): break
            tot = h[mask].sum()
            if tot < 1e-15: break
            h[mask] *= (target/tot)  # since target<tot
        # The multiplication method may not reduce by exactly diff? It scales to target among mask, zero unchanged; sum target if target<tot. exact.
    # final residual correction
    for _ in range(20):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14: break
        if diff > 0:
            idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-h))
            add=min(diff, 1.0-h[idx])
            h[idx]+=add
        else:
            idx=int(np.argmax(h))
            sub=min(-diff, h[idx])
            h[idx]-=sub
    return np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
The scaling for diff<0: if mask includes all positive, h[mask]*=target/tot, sum target exactly, but if target/tot >1? diff<0 => target<tot, factor <1. Good. However if some zeros, they remain. It's exact but may alter relative values. Fine.
The diff>0 method: h[mask] += (diff/room)*(1-h[mask]) increases sum by diff. If diff/room >1, then some exceed 1? If room < diff, impossible because room is total possible; diff ≤ room for feasible. Floating maybe. If diff/room=1, all to 1. Good.

Interpolation:
def resample_h(h,n_new):
   n_old=len(h)
   if n_new == n_old: return h.copy()
   x_old=(np.arange(n_old)+0.5)*(2.0/n_old)
   x_new=(np.arange(n_new)+0.5)*(2.0/n_new)
   h_new=np.interp(x_new,x_old,h)
   return project_feasible(h_new,n_new)
Maybe use mode? Values between. Good.

Smooth:
def smooth_h(h,w):
   if w<=1: return h.copy()
   k=np.ones(w)/w
   hs=np.convolve(h,k,mode='same')
   # np.convolve 'same' for odd/even? It centers. Sum not preserved. project.
   return project_feasible(hs,len(h))
Maybe edges get lower values. Good.

def perturb_h(h, scale, rng):
   return project_feasible(h + rng.normal(0.0, scale, len(h)), len(h))

Binary:
def binary_cut(h):
   n=len(h)
   # We need n/2 entries 1. If n even, exactly. If odd, target n/2 not integer; can set floor to 1 and one half.
   if n % 2 == 0:
       b=np.zeros(n); idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1]; b[idx[:n//2]]=1.0; return b
   else:
       # choose floor(n/2) ones, ceil(n/2) zeros, and one 0.5? target=(n//2)+0.5.
       b=np.zeros(n); k=n//2
       idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1]
       b[idx[:k]]=1.0
       b[idx[k]]+=0.5
       return b
This preserves sum. Evaluate.

def select_shifts(h, topk=5, time_limit=None):

    else: hs=h

    best.append((val,s))
    if time and time.time()>time: break
  best.sort()
  return best[:topk]

Detailed function signatures:
EVAL_FRACTIONS = (1.0, 0.5, 0.1)

def corr_full(h): return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
def objective_cmax(c): return float(c.max())

def eval_move(c, U, i, j, amt, n, temp, diff): ...
def eval_direction(c, U, i, j, sign, maxd, base, n, temp, diff): ...
def refine_direction(c, U, i, j, sign, maxd, n, temp, diff): ...
def scan_pairs(h, c, U, best_cmax, base, n, L, temp, diff, deadline): ...
def update_U_for_change(U, p, delta, rows, idxA, idxB): ... maybe top-level inline in apply.
def apply_move(h, c, U, i, j, amt, rows, idxA, idxB, n): ...

Implementation of local_polish:
def local_polish(h, c, U, deadline):
    n = len(h); dx = 2.0/n; L=2*n-1
    # ensure h/c/U exact? maybe project? But if c/U built.
    best_cmax = float(c.max())
    best_C = best_cmax * dx
    temp = np.empty(L, dtype=float); diff = np.empty(L, dtype=float)
    rows = np.arange(n)
    p = rows[:, None]; r = rows[None, :]
    idxA = (n - 1 + r - p)
    idxB = idxA.T
    base = 0.05
    no_streak = 0
    moves_since_recompute = 0
    while time.time() < deadline and base >= 5e-6:
        move = scan_pairs(h, c, U, best_cmax, base, n, L, temp, diff, deadline)
        if move is None:
            no_streak += 1
            if base <= 1e-4 and no_streak >= 2:
                break
            base *= 0.5
            continue
        i,j,amt,probe_val = move
        # bound maxd
        if amt > 0:
            sign=1
            up = 1.0 - h[i]; if up < 0: up=0
            down = h[j]; if down <0: down=0
            maxd = up if up < down else down
        else:
            sign=-1
            up = 1.0 - h[j]; if up<0: up=0
            down = h[i]; if down<0: down=0
            maxd = up if up < down else down
        if maxd <= MIN_AMT:
            no_streak+=1; base*=0.5; continue
        best_amt=amt; best_val=probe_val
        # refine if worthwhile
        if time.time() < deadline - 0.2 and maxd > 1e-6 and (best_cmax - probe_val) > 1e-9:
            ref_amt, ref_val = refine_direction(c,U,i,j,sign,maxd,n,temp,diff)
            if ref_val < best_val:
                best_val=ref_val; best_amt=ref_amt
        # clamp
        amt_final = clamp_amt(h,i,j,best_amt)
        if amt_final == 0.0 or best_cmax - best_val < 1e-12:
            no_streak += 1
            if base <= 1e-4 and no_streak >= 2: break
            base *= 0.5
            continue
        apply_move(h,c,U,i,j,amt_final,rows,idxA,idxB,n)
        moves_since_recompute += 1
        best_cmax = float(c.max())
        best_C = best_cmax * dx
        no_streak = 0
        if moves_since_recompute >= 200:
            # recompute to remove drift
            c = corr_full(h)
            U = build_U(h)
            best_cmax = float(c.max())
            best_C = best_cmax * dx
            moves_since_recompute = 0
        # If time left small? loop condition.
    # maybe final recompute
    c = corr_full(h)
    best_cmax = float(c.max())
    U = build_U(h) # maybe not needed but return
    best_C = best_cmax * dx
    return h, c, U, best_C

def eval_prepared(c, diff, amt, idx0, idxij, idxji, n, temp):

def initial_fallback(seed):
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   starts=[]
   n=200
   for m in [1,2,3,5]:
      phase=np.linspace(0,2*np.pi,n)
      for a in [0.3,0.45]:
         h=0.5 + a*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*np.arange(n)/n + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
         h=np.clip(h,0,1); project
         starts.append(h)
   for scale in [0.1,0.2,0.4]:
      h=0.5 + rng.normal(0,scale,n); project
   # binary random half
   for _ in range(2):
      h=np.zeros(n); idx=rng.choice(n,n//2,replace=False); h[idx]=1; project
   evaluate quick, return best.

```
import numpy as np
import time

EVAL_FRACTIONS = (1.0, 0.5, 0.1)
MIN_AMT = 1e-10
TINY_AMT = 1e-5
SCAN_TOL = 1e-12

def corr_full(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')

def build_U(h): ...

def project_feasible(h,n): ...

def eval_prepared(c, diff, amt, idx0, idxij, idxji, temp):
    np.multiply(diff, amt, out=temp)
    np.add(c, temp, out=temp)
    a2=amt*amt
    temp[idx0] -= 2.0*a2
    temp[idxij] += a2
    if idxji != idxij:
        temp[idxji] += a2
    return float(np.max(temp))
```
Need pass n? idx0 enough; idx out of range? precomputed. L not. Good.

```
def refine_direction(c,U,i,j,sign,maxd,n,temp,diff):
    if maxd <= MIN_AMT: return 0, inf
    np.subtract(U[i],U[j],out=diff)
    idx0=n-1; idxij=...; idxji=...
    a=0.0; b=maxd
    phi=0.6180339887498949
    c1 = a + phi*(b-a); c2 = b - phi*(b-a)
    f1=eval_prepared(... sign*c1); f2=...
    for _ in range(28):
       if f1 < f2:
          b=c2; c2=c1; f2=f1
          c1=a+phi*(b-a)
          f1=eval_prepared(...)
       else:
          a=c1; c1=c2; f1=f2
          c2=b-phi*(b-a)
          f2=eval_prepared(...)
    t=(a+b)*0.5
    ft=eval_prepared(sign*t)
    # Maybe also check full and half? Convex golden okay. But if f has minimum at endpoint b and due numeric? It will converge to b? For convex increasing then decreasing? No convex. If min at b, f1 < f2? Suppose f increasing on [0,b] (min at 0), f1<f2, b shrinks to c2; eventually near 0. Good. If min at b (decreasing), f1 > f2, a=c1; near b. Good.
    return sign*t, ft
```

`scan_pairs`:
```
def scan_pairs(h,c,U,best_cmax,base,n,L,temp,diff,deadline):
    best_val=best_cmax; bi=-1; bj=-1; ban=0.0
    for i in range(n-1):
        if time.time() > deadline: break
        hi = h[i]
        if hi < 0.0: hi = 0.0
        elif hi > 1.0: hi = 1.0
        up_i = 1.0 - hi
        if up_i < 0.0: up_i = 0.0
        for j in range(i+1,n):
            hj = h[j]
            if hj < 0.0: hj=0.0
            elif hj > 1.0: hj=1.0
            maxp = up_i if up_i < hj else hj
            if maxp > MIN_AMT:
                amt, val = eval_direction(...,1,maxp,base,...)
                if amt != 0.0 and val < best_val - SCAN_TOL:
                    best_val=val; bi=i; bj=j; ban=amt
            # negative
            up_j = 1.0 - hj
            if up_j <0: up_j=0
            maxn = hi if hi < up_j else up_j
            if maxn > MIN_AMT:
                amt,val = eval_direction(...,-1,maxn,...)
                if amt !=0 and val < best_val - SCAN_TOL: ...
    if bi < 0: return None
    return bi,bj,ban,best_val
```
The `hi` clipped each i; if h outside tiny, okay. But if h[i] changed to 1+eps, up_i negative ->0; maxn=hi clipped 1, maybe negative direction. Fine.
Function calls eval_direction 2 per pair. If base very small, trials maybe all duplicates full; full moves always evaluated. Good.

Could speed by skipping eval_direction if current lower bound? Not.

`apply_move`:
```
def apply_move(h,c,U,i,j,amt,rows,idxA,idxB,n):
    if amt == 0.0: return
    idx0=n-1
    c += amt * (U[i] - U[j])
    a2=amt*amt
    c[idx0] -= 2.0*a2
    idxij=idx0+i-j
    c[idxij] += a2
    idxji=idx0+j-i
    if idxji != idxij: c[idxji] += a2
    h[i] += amt
    h[j] -= amt
    # U updates
    U[rows, idxA[i]] -= amt
    U[rows, idxB[i]] -= amt
    U[rows, idxA[j]] += amt
    U[rows, idxB[j]] += amt
```
No clipping. If amt clamped, okay. After h update, sum may drift due rounding. Good.
Potential issue: advanced indexing `U[rows, idxA[i]] -= amt` with repeated indices? For a fixed i, idxA[i] has all distinct? idxA[i,r]=n-1+r-i is strictly increasing in r, distinct. idxB[i] distinct decreasing. The two arrays may have same value at r=i; but separate statements subtract twice, correct. However if using `-=` with advanced indexing, duplicated indices within the same statement? There are none. Good.

`select_shifts`:
```
def select_shifts(h, topk=5, time_limit=None):
   n=len(h); dx=2.0/n
   arr=[]
   for s in range(n):
       if time_limit is not None and time.time() > time_limit: break
       if s == 0:
           c=corr_full(h)
       else:
           c=corr_full(np.roll(h,s))
       arr.append((float(c.max()*dx), s))
   arr.sort(key=lambda x:x[0])  # no lambdas! Use arr.sort() since tuples (value,s).
   return arr[:topk]
```

```
def fallback_h(seed):
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   n=200
   best_h=None; best_c=inf
   starts=[]
   for m in [1,2,3,4,6]:
       x=np.arange(n)
       for amp in [0.2,0.35,0.5]:
           for ph in [0.0, np.pi/2, np.pi]:
              h=0.5 + amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x/n + ph)
              starts.append(h)
   for scale in [0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8]:
       starts.append(0.5 + rng.normal(0,scale,n))
   for _ in range(3):
       h=np.zeros(n); idx=rng.choice(n,n//2,replace=False); h[idx]=1; starts.append(h)
   for h0 in starts:
       h1=project_feasible(np.clip(h0,0,1),n)
       c=corr_full(h1); C=c.max()*2/n
       if C<best_c: best_c=C; best_h=h1
   return best_h
```

```
    # active
    thr = cmax - max(1e-8, cmax*1e-6)
    active = np.where(c >= thr)[0]

       active = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])

       active = active[order]
```

Implement try_gradient_direction top-level:
```
def try_gradient_direction(h, c, U, cmax):
    n=len(h)
    if n == 0: return None
    active = np.where(c >= cmax - max(1e-8, cmax*1e-6))[0]
    if active.size == 0: active=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
    if active.size > 40:
       order=np.argsort(c[active])[::-1][:40]
       active=active[order]
    g=U[:, active].sum(axis=1)
    g -= 0  # no
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)): return None
    d = -g
    d -= d.mean()
    for _ in range(6):
        d[(d < 0) & (h <= 1e-12)] = 0.0
        d[(d > 0) & (h >= 1.0 - 1e-12)] = 0.0
        d -= d.mean()
    ms = np.max(np.abs(d))
    if ms <= 1e-12: return None
    d = d / ms
    # alpha_max
    pos = d > 0; neg = d < 0
    alpha_max = 0.05
    if pos.any():
        room = (1.0 - h[pos]) / d[pos]
        alpha_max = min(alpha_max, float(np.min(room)))
    if neg.any():
        room = h[neg] / (-d[neg])
        alpha_max = min(alpha_max, float(np.min(room)))
    if not np.isfinite(alpha_max) or alpha_max <= 1e-7:
        # if all at bound, try small perturbation with variables not at bound? skip
        return None
    alpha_max *= 0.999
    # also maybe if alpha_max > 0.1: cap 0.1
    if alpha_max > 0.1: alpha_max=0.1
    res = golden_alpha_full(h, d, 0.0, alpha_max)
    if res is None: return None
    best_t, best_f = res
    if best_f >= cmax - 1e-10 or best_t <= 1e-12:
        return None
    h_trial = h + best_t * d
    # enforce bounds tiny clip
    if h_trial.min() < 0.0 or h_trial.max() > 1.0:
         h_trial = np.clip(h_trial,0.0,1.0)
         # project sum maybe
         h_trial = project_feasible(h_trial,n)
    h[:] = h_trial
    new_c = corr_full(h)
    new_cmax = float(new_c.max())
    if new_cmax >= cmax - 1e-10:
        # revert? We modified h. Need restore original. We didn't save. Save copy before accept.
```

```
def golden_alpha_full(h,d,a,b):
    if b <= 1e-12: return None
    phi=0.618...
    c1=a+phi*(b-a); c2=b-phi*(b-a)
    f1=eval_alpha_full(h,d,c1); f2=eval_alpha_full(h,d,c2)
    best_t = c1 if f1 <= f2 else c2; best_f = min(f1,f2)
    for _ in range(22):
        if f1 < f2:
            b=c2; c2=c1; f2=f1

            c2=b-phi*(b-a)
            f2=eval_alpha_full(...)
```

```
import numpy as np
import time

EVAL_FRACTIONS = (1.0, 0.5, 0.1)
MIN_AMT = 1e-10
TINY_AMT = 1e-5
SCAN_TOL = 1e-12
GRAD_TOL = 1e-10
```

`corr_full`:
```
def corr_full(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
```

`project_feasible` as above. In final if h length 0? no.

Need be careful with `last_t` and `abs(t-last_t) > 1e-14` with t maybe 0? t>MIN_AMT. Good.
```
def eval_direction(...):
    if maxd <= MIN_AMT: return 0.0, float('inf')
    np.subtract(U[i], U[j], out=diff)
    idx0 = n-1; idxij=idx0+i-j; idxji=idx0+j-i
    best_val = float('inf'); best_t=0.0; last_t=-1.0
    for frac in EVAL_FRACTIONS:
       t = maxd * frac
       if t <= MIN_AMT: break
       if abs(t - last_t) > 1e-14:
           amt = sign * t
           v = eval_prepared(c, diff, amt, idx0, idxij, idxji, temp)
           last_t = t
           if v < best_val:
              best_val = v; best_t = t
    t = base if base < maxd else maxd
    if t > MIN_AMT and abs(t-last_t)>1e-14:
       ...
    t = base*0.2 if base*0.2 < maxd else maxd
    ...
    t = TINY_AMT if TINY_AMT < maxd else maxd
    ...
    if best_t == 0.0: return 0.0, float('inf')
    return sign*best_t, best_val
```

```
def local_polish(h, c, U, deadline):
    n=len(h); dx=2.0/n; L=2*n-1
    h = np.clip(h,0,1) # maybe copy? We want modify in place? If h passed copy, ok.
    c = corr_full(h)
    U = build_U(h)
    best_cmax = float(c.max())
    best_C = best_cmax*dx
    temp=np.empty(L); diff=np.empty(L)
    rows=np.arange(n)
    pvec=rows[:,None]; rvec=rows[None,:]
    idxA = (n-1 + rvec - pvec)
    idxB = idxA.T
    base=0.05
    no_streak=0
    moves_since=0
    while time.time() < deadline and base >= 5e-6:
        move = scan_pairs(..., deadline)
        if move is not None:
            i,j,amt,probe_val = move
            ...
            if apply:
               apply_move(...)
               moves_since +=1
               best_cmax = float(c.max())
               # maybe if best_cmax > best_C/dx + 1e-9 due drift? recompute if
               if best_cmax > best_C/dx + 1e-8:
                   c=corr_full(h); U=build_U(h); best_cmax=c.max()
               ...
               no_streak=0
               if moves_since >= 100:
                   c=corr_full(h); U=build_U(h); best_cmax=c.max(); moves_since=0
               continue
        # no pair move
        if base >= 1e-5 and time.time() < deadline - 1:
            res = try_gradient_direction(h,c,U,best_cmax)
            if res is not None:
                new_c,new_U,new_cmax=res
                if new_cmax < best_cmax - 1e-12:
                    c=new_c; U=new_U; best_cmax=new_cmax; best_C=...
                    moves_since=0; no_streak=0; continue
        no_streak += 1
        if base <= 1e-4 and no_streak >= 3: break
        base *= 0.5
    c=corr_full(h); U=build_U(h); best_cmax=float(c.max()); best_C=best_cmax*dx
    return h,c,U,best_C
```

`clamp_move_amt` top-level:
```
def clamp_move_amt(h,i,j,amt):
    if amt > 0:
        m1=1.0-h[i]; m2=h[j]
        if m1 < 0: m1=0; if m2<0:m2=0
        mx = m1 if m1 < m2 else m2
        if amt > mx: amt = mx
        if amt < 0: amt = 0
        eps=1e-13
        if amt > 0:
           # keep tiny margin
           hi_new = h[i] + amt
           hj_new = h[j] - amt
           # if hi_new > 1 - 1e-13 or hj_new < 1e-13? Maybe reduce to max 0.
           if hi_new > 1.0: amt = max(0.0, 1.0 - h[i])
           if hj_new < 0.0: amt = min(amt, h[j])
    elif amt < 0:
        m1=1.0-h[j]; m2=h[i]
        ... amt = -min(...)
    if abs(amt) < 1e-15: return 0.0
    return amt
```
We don't need margin if apply doesn't clip. But to avoid out, use if h[i]+amt not in [0,1] reduce. Use simple:
```
if amt > 0:
   if h[i] + amt > 1.0: amt = 1.0 - h[i]
   if h[j] - amt < 0.0: amt = h[j]
   if amt < 0: amt=0
elif amt < 0:
   if h[i] + amt < 0.0: amt = -h[i]
   if h[j] - amt > 1.0: amt = -(1.0 - h[j])
   if amt > 0: amt=0
```
If due floating h[i] already 1, amt 0. Good. No margin; apply h may become exactly 1. Fine.

After apply, if h outside due rounding? maybe not. If h[i] =1 and amt=1-h[i] exactly? h[i]+amt might be 1.0000000000000002. Could final project. In local search, such tiny out may affect maxp. Recompute every 100 may project? build_U doesn't clip; U formula okay even if 1+eps? c from np.correlate with 1+eps maybe slightly. To be safe, after apply, if h[i]<0 or >1 or h[j]<0 or >1:
```
h=np.clip(h,0,1)
```

```
def quick_c(h):
```

`select_shifts` uses no lambda.
```
def select_shifts(h, topk=6, time_limit=None):
    n=len(h); dx=2.0/n; arr=[]
    for s in range(n):
        if time_limit is not None and time.time() > time_limit: break
        if s == 0:
            c=corr_full(h)
        else:
            c=corr_full(np.roll(h, s))
        arr.append((float(c.max()*dx), s))
    arr.sort()
    return arr[:topk]
```
If time_limit breaks before all, arr sorted partial. Fine.

```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    B=float(budget_s)
    t_dead = t0 + max(10.0, B - 25.0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    # load
    try:
       h = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
       if h.size == 0: raise ValueError
    except Exception:
       h = fallback_h(seed)
    n = len(h)
    h = project_feasible(h,n)
    c = corr_full(h)
    C = float(c.max()*2.0/n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_C=C; best_n=n
    # initial local
    init_alloc = min(500.0, max(30.0, 0.45*B))
    end1 = t0 + init_alloc
    if end1 > t_dead - 80:
        end1 = max(t0 + 5.0, t_dead - 80)
    if end1 > t0 + 5.0:
       h_c = best_h.copy()
       h_c,C_c,_=polish_candidate(h_c,end1)
       if C_c < best_C:
           best_h=h_c; best_C=C_c; best_n=len(h_c)
    # shifts
    if best_n <= 400 and time.time() < t_dead - 120:
       limit = time.time() + min(30.0, max(5.0, (t_dead - time.time()) * 0.04))
       shifts = select_shifts(best_h, topk=6, time_limit=limit)
       for C_s,s in shifts:
           if time.time() > t_dead - 100: break
           if s == 0: continue
           if C_s > best_C + 0.02: continue
           h_c = np.roll(best_h, s)
           h_c = project_feasible(h_c, best_n)
           alloc = min(60.0, max(15.0, (t_dead - time.time() - 80) * 0.25))
           if time.time() + alloc > t_dead - 80: alloc = max(0, t_dead - 80 - time.time())
           if alloc < 15: break
           h_c,C_c,n_c=polish_candidate(h_c, time.time()+alloc)
           if C_c < best_C:
               best_h=h_c; best_C=C_c; best_n=n_c
    # resample
    if time.time() < t_dead - 100:
       base_n=best_n
       ns=[]
       for x in [base_n, base_n+1, base_n+5, base_n+15, base_n+25, int(1.2*base_n), 300, 350]:
           if x < 80 or x > 500: continue
           if x not in ns: ns.append(x)
       ns.sort()
       for n_new in ns:
           if time.time() > t_dead - 90: break

               # maybe re-polish current with remaining fraction
               alloc = min(120.0, max(0.0, t_dead - 90 - time.time())*0.2)

               best_h=h_c; best_C=C_c; best_n=n_c
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time

EVAL_FRACTIONS = (1.0, 0.5, 0.1)
MIN_AMT = 1e-10
TINY_AMT = 1e-5
SCAN_TOL = 1e-12
GRAD_TOL = 1e-10


def corr_full(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")


def quick_c(h):
    c = corr_full(h)
    return float(c.max() * (2.0 / len(h)))


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * n

    for _ in range(1000):
        s = float(h.sum())
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            mask = h < 1.0
            if not np.any(mask):
                break
            room = float((1.0 - h[mask]).sum())
            if room < 1e-15:
                break
            h[mask] += (diff / room) * (1.0 - h[mask])
        else:
            mask = h > 0.0
            if not np.any(mask):
                break
            tot = float(h[mask].sum())
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h[mask] *= (target / tot)

    for _ in range(30):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            add = min(diff, 1.0 - h[idx])
            h[idx] += add
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            sub = min(-diff, h[idx])
            h[idx] -= sub

    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)


def build_U(h):
    n = len(h)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    U = np.zeros((n, L), dtype=float)
    rev_one = np.ascontiguousarray((1.0 - h)[::-1])
    neg_h = np.ascontiguousarray(-h)

    for r in range(n):
        U[r, r:r + n] += rev_one
        U[r, n - 1 - r:2 * n - 1 - r] += neg_h
    return U


def eval_prepared(c, diff, amt, idx0, idxij, idxji, temp):
    np.multiply(diff, amt, out=temp)
    np.add(c, temp, out=temp)
    a2 = amt * amt
    temp[idx0] -= 2.0 * a2
    temp[idxij] += a2
    if idxji != idxij:
        temp[idxji] += a2
    return float(np.max(temp))


def eval_direction(c, U, i, j, sign, maxd, base, n, temp, diff):
    if maxd <= MIN_AMT:
        return 0.0, float("inf")

    np.subtract(U[i], U[j], out=diff)
    idx0 = n - 1
    idxij = idx0 + i - j
    idxji = idx0 + j - i

    best_val = float("inf")
    best_t = 0.0
    last_t = -1.0

    for frac in EVAL_FRACTIONS:
        t = maxd * frac
        if t <= MIN_AMT:
            break
        if abs(t - last_t) > 1e-14:
            amt = sign * t
            v = eval_prepared(c, diff, amt, idx0, idxij, idxji, temp)
            last_t = t
            if v < best_val:
                best_val = v
                best_t = t

    t = base if base < maxd else maxd
    if t > MIN_AMT and abs(t - last_t) > 1e-14:
        amt = sign * t
        v = eval_prepared(c, diff, amt, idx0, idxij, idxji, temp)
        last_t = t
        if v < best_val:
            best_val = v
            best_t = t

    bs = base * 0.2
    t = bs if bs < maxd else maxd
    if t > MIN_AMT and abs(t - last_t) > 1e-14:
        amt = sign * t
        v = eval_prepared(c, diff, amt, idx0, idxij, idxji, temp)
        last_t = t
        if v < best_val:
            best_val = v
            best_t = t

    t = TINY_AMT if TINY_AMT < maxd else maxd
    if t > MIN_AMT and abs(t - last_t) > 1e-14:
        amt = sign * t
        v = eval_prepared(c, diff, amt, idx0, idxij, idxji, temp)
        last_t = t
        if v < best_val:
            best_val = v
            best_t = t

    if best_t == 0.0:
        return 0.0, float("inf")
    return sign * best_t, best_val


def refine_direction(c, U, i, j, sign, maxd, n, temp, diff):
    if maxd <= MIN_AMT:
        return 0.0, float("inf")

    np.subtract(U[i], U[j], out=diff)
    idx0 = n - 1
    idxij = idx0 + i - j
    idxji = idx0 + j - i

    a = 0.0
    b = float(maxd)
    phi = 0.6180339887498949

    c1 = a + phi * (b - a)
    c2 = b - phi * (b - a)
    f1 = eval_prepared(c, diff, sign * c1, idx0, idxij, idxji, temp)
    f2 = eval_prepared(c, diff, sign * c2, idx0, idxij, idxji, temp)

    best_t = c1 if f1 <= f2 else c2
    best_f = f1 if f1 <= f2 else f2

    for _ in range(24):
        if f1 < f2:
            b = c2
            c2 = c1
            f2 = f1
            c1 = a + phi * (b - a)
            f1 = eval_prepared(c, diff, sign * c1, idx0, idxij, idxji, temp)
        else:
            a = c1
            c1 = c2
            f1 = f2
            c2 = b - phi * (b - a)
            f2 = eval_prepared(c, diff, sign * c2, idx0, idxij, idxji, temp)

        if f1 < best_f:
            best_f = f1
            best_t = c1
        if f2 < best_f:
            best_f = f2
            best_t = c2

    t = 0.5 * (a + b)
    f = eval_prepared(c, diff, sign * t, idx0, idxij, idxji, temp)
    if f < best_f:
        best_f = f
        best_t = t

    return sign * best_t, best_f


def scan_pairs(h, c, U, best_cmax, base, n, L, temp, diff, deadline):
    best_val = float(best_cmax)
    bi = -1
    bj = -1
    ban = 0.0

    for i in range(n - 1):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break

        hi = h[i]
        if hi < 0.0:
            hi = 0.0
        elif hi > 1.0:
            hi = 1.0
        up_i = 1.0 - hi
        if up_i < 0.0:
            up_i = 0.0

        for j in range(i + 1, n):
            if (j & 31) == 31 and time.time() > deadline:
                break

            hj = h[j]
            if hj < 0.0:
                hj = 0.0
            elif hj > 1.0:
                hj = 1.0

            maxp = up_i if up_i < hj else hj
            if maxp > MIN_AMT:
                amt, val = eval_direction(c, U, i, j, 1, maxp, base, n, temp, diff)
                if amt != 0.0 and val < best_val - SCAN_TOL:
                    best_val = val
                    bi = i
                    bj = j
                    ban = amt

            up_j = 1.0 - hj
            if up_j < 0.0:
                up_j = 0.0
            maxn = hi if hi < up_j else up_j
            if maxn > MIN_AMT:
                amt, val = eval_direction(c, U, i, j, -1, maxn, base, n, temp, diff)
                if amt != 0.0 and val < best_val - SCAN_TOL:
                    best_val = val
                    bi = i
                    bj = j
                    ban = amt

    if bi < 0:
        return None
    return bi, bj, ban, best_val


def clamp_move_amt(h, i, j, amt):
    eps = 1e-13
    if amt > 0.0:
        m1 = 1.0 - h[i] - eps
        m2 = h[j] - eps
        if m1 < 0.0:
            m1 = 0.0
        if m2 < 0.0:
            m2 = 0.0
        mx = m1 if m1 < m2 else m2
        if amt > mx:
            amt = mx
        if amt < 0.0:
            amt = 0.0
    elif amt < 0.0:
        m1 = 1.0 - h[j] - eps
        m2 = h[i] - eps
        if m1 < 0.0:
            m1 = 0.0
        if m2 < 0.0:
            m2 = 0.0
        mx = m1 if m1 < m2 else m2
        if -amt > mx:
            amt = -mx
        if amt > 0.0:
            amt = 0.0

    if abs(amt) < 1e-15:
        return 0.0
    return amt


def apply_move(h, c, U, i, j, amt, rows, idxA, idxB, n):
    if amt == 0.0:
        return

    idx0 = n - 1
    c += amt * (U[i] - U[j])
    a2 = amt * amt
    c[idx0] -= 2.0 * a2

    idxij = idx0 + i - j
    c[idxij] += a2
    idxji = idx0 + j - i
    if idxji != idxij:
        c[idxji] += a2

    h[i] += amt
    h[j] -= amt

    U[rows, idxA[i]] -= amt
    U[rows, idxB[i]] -= amt
    U[rows, idxA[j]] += amt
    U[rows, idxB[j]] += amt


def eval_alpha_full(h, d, alpha):
    ht = h + alpha * d
    if ht.min() < -1e-9 or ht.max() > 1.0 + 1e-9:
        return float("inf")
    c = corr_full(ht)
    return float(c.max())


def golden_alpha_full(h, d, a, b):
    if b <= 1e-12:
        return None

    phi = 0.6180339887498949
    c1 = a + phi * (b - a)
    c2 = b - phi * (b - a)
    f1 = eval_alpha_full(h, d, c1)
    f2 = eval_alpha_full(h, d, c2)

    best_t = c1 if f1 <= f2 else c2
    best_f = f1 if f1 <= f2 else f2

    for _ in range(22):
        if f1 < f2:
            b = c2
            c2 = c1
            f2 = f1
            c1 = a + phi * (b - a)
            f1 = eval_alpha_full(h, d, c1)
        else:
            a = c1
            c1 = c2
            f1 = f2
            c2 = b - phi * (b - a)
            f2 = eval_alpha_full(h, d, c2)

        if f1 < best_f:
            best_f = f1
            best_t = c1
        if f2 < best_f:
            best_f = f2
            best_t = c2

    t = 0.5 * (a + b)
    f = eval_alpha_full(h, d, t)
    if f < best_f:
        best_f = f
        best_t = t

    return best_t, best_f


def try_gradient_direction(h, c, U, cmax):
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return None

    tol = max(1e-8, cmax * 1e-6)
    active = np.where(c >= cmax - tol)[0]
    if active.size == 0:
        active = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)
    elif active.size > 40:
        order = np.argsort(c[active])[::-1][:40]
        active = active[order]

    g = U[:, active].sum(axis=1)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        return None

    d = -g
    d -= d.mean()

    for _ in range(6):
        d[(d < 0.0) & (h <= 1e-12)] = 0.0
        d[(d > 0.0) & (h >= 1.0 - 1e-12)] = 0.0
        d -= d.mean()

    ms = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
    if ms <= 1e-12:
        return None
    d = d / ms

    pos = d > 0.0
    neg = d < 0.0
    alpha_max = 0.05
    if pos.any():
        room = (1.0 - h[pos]) / d[pos]
        rmin = float(np.min(room))
        if rmin < alpha_max:
            alpha_max = rmin
    if neg.any():
        room = h[neg] / (-d[neg])
        rmin = float(np.min(room))
        if rmin < alpha_max:
            alpha_max = rmin

    if (not np.isfinite(alpha_max)) or alpha_max <= 1e-8:
        return None

    alpha_max *= 0.999
    if alpha_max > 0.1:
        alpha_max = 0.1

    res = golden_alpha_full(h, d, 0.0, alpha_max)
    if res is None:
        return None

    best_t, best_f = res
    if best_f >= cmax - 1e-10 or best_t <= 1e-12:
        return None

    h_trial = h + best_t * d
    if h_trial.min() < 0.0 or h_trial.max() > 1.0:
        h_trial = np.clip(h_trial, 0.0, 1.0)
        h_trial = project_feasible(h_trial, n)

    c_trial = corr_full(h_trial)
    new_cmax = float(c_trial.max())
    if new_cmax >= cmax - 1e-10:
        return None

    h[:] = h_trial
    U_trial = build_U(h)
    return c_trial, U_trial, new_cmax


def local_polish(h, c, U, deadline):
    n = len(h)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = corr_full(h)
    U = build_U(h)

    best_cmax = float(c.max())
    best_C = best_cmax * dx
    L = 2 * n - 1

    temp = np.empty(L, dtype=float)
    diff = np.empty(L, dtype=float)

    rows = np.arange(n)
    pvec = rows[:, None]
    rvec = rows[None, :]
    idxA = n - 1 + rvec - pvec
    idxB = idxA.T

    base = 0.05
    no_streak = 0
    moves_since_recompute = 0

    while time.time() < deadline and base >= 5e-6:
        move = scan_pairs(h, c, U, best_cmax, base, n, L, temp, diff, deadline)

        accepted = False
        if move is not None:
            i, j, amt, probe_val = move

            if amt > 0.0:
                sign = 1
                up = 1.0 - h[i]
                if up < 0.0:
                    up = 0.0
                down = h[j]
                if down < 0.0:
                    down = 0.0
                maxd = up if up < down else down
            else:
                sign = -1
                up = 1.0 - h[j]
                if up < 0.0:
                    up = 0.0
                down = h[i]
                if down < 0.0:
                    down = 0.0
                maxd = up if up < down else down

            if maxd > MIN_AMT:
                best_amt = amt
                best_val = probe_val

                if time.time() < deadline - 0.2 and maxd > 1e-6 and (best_cmax - probe_val) > 1e-9:
                    ref_amt, ref_val = refine_direction(c, U, i, j, sign, maxd, n, temp, diff)
                    if ref_val < best_val:
                        best_val = ref_val
                        best_amt = ref_amt

                final_amt = clamp_move_amt(h, i, j, best_amt)
                if final_amt != 0.0 and best_val < best_cmax - SCAN_TOL:
                    apply_move(h, c, U, i, j, final_amt, rows, idxA, idxB, n)
                    moves_since_recompute += 1
                    best_cmax = float(c.max())

                    if best_cmax > best_C / dx + 1e-8:
                        c = corr_full(h)
                        U = build_U(h)
                        best_cmax = float(c.max())

                    best_C = best_cmax * dx
                    no_streak = 0
                    accepted = True

                    if moves_since_recompute >= 100:
                        c = corr_full(h)
                        U = build_U(h)
                        best_cmax = float(c.max())
                        best_C = best_cmax * dx
                        moves_since_recompute = 0

        if not accepted:
            if base >= 1e-5 and time.time() < deadline - 1.0:
                res = try_gradient_direction(h, c, U, best_cmax)
                if res is not None:
                    new_c, new_U, new_cmax = res
                    if new_cmax < best_cmax - 1e-12:
                        c = new_c
                        U = new_U
                        best_cmax = new_cmax
                        best_C = best_cmax * dx
                        moves_since_recompute = 0
                        no_streak = 0
                        continue

            no_streak += 1
            if base <= 1e-4 and no_streak >= 3:
                break
            base *= 0.5

    c = corr_full(h)
    U = build_U(h)
    best_cmax = float(c.max())
    best_C = best_cmax * dx
    return h, c, U, best_C


def polish_candidate(h, deadline):
    n = len(h)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c0 = quick_c(h)
    if time.time() > deadline:
        return h, c0, n

    c = corr_full(h)
    U = build_U(h)
    h, c, U, C = local_polish(h, c, U, deadline)
    return h, C, n


def resample_h(h, n_new):
    n_old = len(h)
    if n_new == n_old:
        return h.copy()

    x_old = (np.arange(n_old, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_old)
    x_new = (np.arange(n_new, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_new)
    h_new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)
    return project_feasible(h_new, n_new)


def smooth_h(h, w):
    if w <= 1:
        return h.copy()
    if w > len(h):
        w = len(h)
    k = np.ones(w, dtype=float) / float(w)
    hs = np.convolve(h, k, mode="same")
    return project_feasible(hs, len(h))


def symmetrize_h(h):
    return project_feasible(0.5 * (h + h[::-1]), len(h))


def perturb_h(h, scale, rng):
    return project_feasible(h + rng.normal(0.0, scale, len(h)), len(h))


def binary_cut(h):
    n = len(h)
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    idx = np.argsort(h)[::-1]
    if n % 2 == 0:
        b[idx[:n // 2]] = 1.0
    else:
        k = n // 2
        b[idx[:k]] = 1.0
        if idx.size > k:
            b[idx[k]] = 0.5
    return project_feasible(b, n)


def select_shifts(h, topk=6, time_limit=None):
    n = len(h)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    arr = []
    for s in range(n):
        if time_limit is not None and time.time() > time_limit:
            break
        if s == 0:
            c = corr_full(h)
        else:
            c = corr_full(np.roll(h, s))
        arr.append((float(c.max() * dx), s))
    arr.sort()
    return arr[:topk]


def fallback_h(seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    n = 200
    starts = []

    for m in (1, 2, 3, 4, 6):
        base = 0.5 + (2.0 * np.pi * m * np.arange(n, dtype=float) / float(n))
        for amp in (0.2, 0.35, 0.48):
            for ph in (0.0, 0.5 * np.pi, np.pi):
                starts.append(0.5 + amp * np.sin(base + ph))

    for scale in (0.1, 0.2, 0.4, 0.8):
        starts.append(0.5 + rng.normal(0.0, scale, n))

    for _ in range(3):
        b = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        idx = rng.choice(n, n // 2, replace=False)
        b[idx] = 1.0
        starts.append(b)

    best_h = None
    best_c = float("inf")
    for x in starts:
        hh = project_feasible(x, n)
        cc = quick_c(hh)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_c = cc
            best_h = hh

    if best_h is None:
        best_h = project_feasible(np.full(n, 0.5), n)
    return best_h


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    B = float(budget_s)
    t_dead = t0 + max(10.0, B - 25.0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h0 = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        if h0.size == 0:
            raise ValueError("empty parent")
    except Exception:
        h0 = fallback_h(seed)

    n0 = len(h0)
    h0 = project_feasible(h0, n0)
    best_C = quick_c(h0)
    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_n = n0

    init_alloc = min(500.0, max(30.0, 0.45 * B))
    end1 = t0 + init_alloc
    if end1 > t_dead - 80.0:
        end1 = max(t0 + 5.0, t_dead - 80.0)

    if end1 > t0 + 5.0:
        hc, Cc, nc = polish_candidate(best_h.copy(), end1)
        if Cc < best_C - 1e-12:
            best_h = hc
            best_C = Cc
            best_n = nc

    if best_n <= 400 and time.time() < t_dead - 120.0:
        lim = time.time() + min(30.0, max(5.0, 0.04 * (t_dead - time.time())))
        shifts = select_shifts(best_h, topk=6, time_limit=lim)
        for Cs, s in shifts:
            if time.time() > t_dead - 100.0:
                break
            if s == 0:
                continue
            if Cs > best_C + 0.02:
                continue

            remaining = t_dead - 80.0 - time.time()
            if remaining < 15.0:
                break
            alloc = min(60.0, 0.25 * remaining)
            if alloc < 15.0:
                break

            hc = np.roll(best_h, s)
            hc = project_feasible(hc, best_n)
            hc, Cc, nc = polish_candidate(hc, time.time() + alloc)
            if Cc < best_C - 1e-12:
                best_h = hc
                best_C = Cc
                best_n = nc

    if time.time() < t_dead - 100.0:
        base_n = best_n
        ns = []
        for x in (base_n, base_n + 1, base_n + 5, base_n + 15, base_n + 25,
                  int(1.2 * base_n), 300, 350):
            if x < 80 or x > 500:
                continue
            if x not in ns:
                ns.append(x)
        ns.sort()

        for n_new in ns:
            if time.time() > t_dead - 90.0:
                break

            remaining = t_dead - 90.0 - time.time()
            if remaining < 20.0:
                break

            if n_new == best_n:
                alloc = min(120.0, 0.20 * remaining)
                if alloc < 20.0:
                    continue
                hc, Cc, nc = polish_candidate(best_h.copy(), time.time() + alloc)
                if Cc < best_C - 1e-12:
                    best_h = hc
                    best_C = Cc
                    best_n = nc
                continue

            hc = resample_h(best_h, n_new)
            C0 = quick_c(hc)
            if C0 > best_C + 0.02 and remaining < 180.0:
                continue

            if n_new <= 300:
                base_alloc = 80.0
            elif n_new <= 400:
                base_alloc = 60.0
            else:
                base_alloc = 40.0

            alloc = min(base_alloc, 0.25 * remaining)
            if alloc < 20.0:
                continue

            hc, Cc, nc = polish_candidate(hc, time.time() + alloc)
            if Cc < best_C - 1e-12:
                best_h = hc
                best_C = Cc
                best_n = nc

    if time.time() < t_dead - 80.0:
        bhc = binary_cut(best_h)
        Cb = quick_c(bhc)
        remaining = t_dead - 70.0 - time.time()
        if Cb < best_C + 0.02 and remaining > 15.0:
            alloc = min(80.0, 0.20 * remaining)
            if alloc > 15.0:
                bhc, Cc, nc = polish_candidate(bhc, time.time() + alloc)
                if Cc < best_C - 1e-12:
                    best_h = bhc
                    best_C = Cc
                    best_n = nc

    if time.time() < t_dead - 80.0:
        candidates = []
        for w in (3, 5):
            candidates.append(smooth_h(best_h, w))
        candidates.append(symmetrize_h(best_h))
        for sc in (0.03, 0.08, 0.15):
            candidates.append(perturb_h(best_h, sc, rng))

        scored = []
        for hc in candidates:
            if time.time() > t_dead - 70.0:
                break
            hc = project_feasible(hc, len(hc))
            C0 = quick_c(hc)
            scored.append((C0, hc))

        scored.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])  # safe tuple sort
        for k, (C0, hc) in enumerate(scored):
            if time.time() > t_dead - 60.0:
                break
            if k >= 3:
                break
            if C0 > best_C + 0.015:
                continue

            remaining = t_dead - 50.0 - time.time()
            if remaining < 10.0:
                break
            alloc = min(40.0, 0.25 * remaining)
            if alloc < 10.0:
                break

            hc, Cc, nc = polish_candidate(hc, time.time() + alloc)
            if Cc < best_C - 1e-12:
                best_h = hc
                best_C = Cc
                best_n = nc

    if time.time() < t_dead - 2.0:
        hc = best_h.copy()
        hc, Cc, nc = polish_candidate(hc, t_dead)
        if Cc < best_C - 1e-12:
            best_h = hc
            best_C = Cc
            best_n = nc

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, len(best_h))
    cf = corr_full(best_h)
    best_C = float(cf.max() * (2.0 / len(best_h)))
    return best_h, best_C, len(best_h)
```
